\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+r^{-n}\|u/r^{s}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}+r^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{2r}(z)).

We then use (
4.9
) and (
4.11
) to see that there is a constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
such that

(4.13)

\displaystyle\|v/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{r/4}(z))}\leq c\lambda.

We now consider the case
d(z)>2r
.
We observe from (
4.7
) and (
4.8
) that

(4.14)

\displaystyle r^{-n}\|u/r^{s}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}+r^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{2r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{cd^{s}(z)}{r^{s}}\left[\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{4r}(z)}|u/d^{s}|\,dx+c\max\{d(z),4r\}^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{4r}(z))\right]\leq\frac{cd^{s}(z)}{r^{s}}\lambda.

Then, using the Sobolev embedding, we get

r^{-n}\|u/d^{s}-v/d^{s}\|_{L^{1}(\Omega\cap B_{r}(z))}\leq\frac{cr^{s}}{d^{s}(z)}r^{-n}\|u/r^{s}-v/r^{s}\|_{L^{1}(\Omega\cap B_{r}(z))}\leq\frac{cr^{s}}{d^{s}(z)}r^{-n/2}[u-v]_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}.

Plugging these inequalities into (
4.10
) yields

(4.15)

\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(z)}|u/d^{s}-v/d^{s}|\,dx\leq c\delta\lambda+c\delta^{\frac{\delta_{0}}{2(1+\delta_{0})}}\lambda\leq c\varepsilon\lambda

by taking
\delta
sufficiently small. Moreover, we also have

(4.16)

\displaystyle\|v/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{r/4}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{cr^{s}}{d^{s}(z)}(r^{-n}\|v/r^{s}\|_{L^{1}(B_{r}(z))}+\mathrm{Tail}(v/r^{s};B_{r}(z)))

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{cr^{s}}{d^{s}(z)}\left(r^{-n}\|u/r^{s}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}+r^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{2r}(z))\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
, where we have used Lemma
2.7
, (
4.9
) and (
4.14
). Therefore combining the estimates (
4.12
), (
4.13
), (
4.15
) and (
4.16
) together with the choice of
\delta=\delta(\mathsf{data},\varepsilon)
determined by (
4.12
) and (
4.15
) yields the desired result.
∎

Let us set

(4.17)

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}\coloneqq

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{1/2}}|u/d^{s}|\,dx+\max\{d(0),1/2\}^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{1/2})+\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{1/2}}|f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}.

For
\lambda\geq\lambda_{1}
and
N\geq 1
, we define

\mathcal{C}\coloneqq\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}|)(z)>N\lambda\}

and

\mathcal{D}\coloneqq\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}|)(z)>\lambda\}\cup\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2_{*}s,2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z)>(\delta\lambda)^{2_{*}}\}.

By the weak 1-1 estimate, we get

(4.18)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}|\leq\frac{c}{N}

for all
\lambda\geq\lambda_{1}
, where
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.

Lemma 4.4
.

Let us fix
z\in{\Omega}\cap B_{1/4}
and
\rho\in(0,2^{-10}]
.
There exists
N\geq 1
so that for any
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, there are
\delta=\delta(\mathsf{data},\varepsilon)\in(0,1)
such that for any
\lambda\geq\lambda_{1}
with
A
being
(\delta,2\rho)
-vanishing, if

|\mathcal{C}\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)|\geq\varepsilon|B_{\rho/8}|,

then we have

\Omega\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)\subset\mathcal{D}.

Proof.

For
\varepsilon_{1}>0
, we select
\delta=\delta(\mathsf{data},\varepsilon_{1})\in(0,1)
as given by Lemma
4.3
. On the contrary, assume that
\Omega\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)\not\subset\mathcal{D}
. This means that there exists
\tilde{x}\in B_{\rho/8}(z)\cap\Omega
so that

(4.19)

\displaystyle\sup_{0<r\leq 2^{-6}}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(\tilde{x})}|u/d^{s}|\,dx\leq\lambda\quad\mbox{and }\sup_{0<r\leq 2^{-6}}\,\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(\tilde{x})}|r^{s}f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\leq(\delta\lambda)^{2_{*}}.

We now verify the hypotheses of Lemma
4.3
by establishing

(4.20)

\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{4\rho}(z)}|u/d^{s}|\,dx+\max\{d(z),4\rho\}^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{4\rho}(z))\leq c\lambda

and

(4.21)

\lambda\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2\rho}(z)}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2\rho}(z)}|A-(A)_{B_{2\rho}(z)\times B_{2\rho}(z)}|\,dx\,dy+\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{4\rho}(z)}(\rho^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\leq c\delta\lambda,

for some
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. We fix a non-negative integer
N_{\rho}
such that

\displaystyle{1}/{16}<2^{N_{\rho}+7}\rho\leq 1/8.

Then, we have the following inclusion

(4.22)

\displaystyle B_{2^{i+2}\rho}(z)\subset B_{2^{i+3}\rho}(\tilde{x})\subset B_{1/2}\quad\mbox{for all }i=0,1,\dots,N_{\rho}+4.

Thus, using (
4.19
) and (
4.22
), we have

(4.23)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{4\rho}(z)}|u/d^{s}|\,dx\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{8\rho}(\tilde{x})}|u/d^{s}|\,dx\leq c\lambda

and

(4.24)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{4\rho}(z)}(\rho^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{8\rho}(\tilde{x})}(\rho^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\leq c\delta\lambda.

Now by
[
DKLN24a
, Lemma 2.2]
, we estimate the nonlocal tail term as

\displaystyle\max\{d(z),4\rho\}^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{4\rho}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{d(z),4\rho\}^{-s}\rho^{2s}\left[\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\rho}}(2^{i}\rho)^{-2s}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{i+2}\rho}(z)}|u|\,dy+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2^{N_{\rho}}\rho}(z)}\frac{|u(y)|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\right]\eqqcolon J_{1}+J_{2}.

Using (
4.7
) and (
4.19
) together with
u\equiv 0
on
B_{1/2}\setminus\Omega
, we further estimate
J_{1}
as

\displaystyle J_{1}\leq c\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-is}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{i+2}\rho}(z)}|u/d^{s}|\,dy\leq c\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-is}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{i+3}\rho}(\widetilde{x})}|u/d^{s}|\,dy\leq c\lambda\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-is}\leq c\lambda

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
, where we have also used (
2.1
) and the fact that
2^{N_{\rho}+3}\rho\leq 2^{-6}
. On the other hand, using (
4.7
) and the fact that
d(0)\leq c
for some constant
c=c(\Omega)
, we estimate
J_{2}
as

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{d(z),4\rho\}^{-s}\rho^{2s}\left[\int_{B_{1/2}\setminus\Omega}|u(y)|\,dy+\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{1/2})\right]

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{1/2}\setminus\Omega}|u/d^{s}|\,dy+\max\{d(0),1/2\}^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{1/2})\right)\leq c\lambda,

where
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Therefore, combining the above displays and noting that
A
is
(\delta,2\rho)
-vanishing (see Definition
1.3
), we observe that (
4.20
) and (
4.21
) hold true. Thus, by Lemma
4.3
with
r
and
\lambda
replaced by
\rho
and
c\lambda
, respectively, we get that there is a function
v
such that

(4.25)

\|v/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{\rho/4}(z))}\leq c\lambda\quad\text{and}\quad\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{\rho}(z)}|u/d^{s}-v/d^{s}|\,dx\leq c\varepsilon_{1}\lambda

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. We next observe

(4.26)

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)\subset\{x\in\Omega\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)\,:\,M_{\rho/16}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}-v/d^{s}|)(x)>\lambda\}

provided that
N
is chosen sufficiently large depending only on the
\mathsf{data}
. Indeed, let
\hat{x}\in\Omega\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)
such that
M_{\rho/16}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}-v/d^{s}|)(\hat{x})\leq\lambda
.

(a)

If
r\in(0,\rho/16)
, then we note from (
4.25
) that there is a constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
such that

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(\hat{x})}|u/d^{s}|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(\hat{x})}|(u-v)/d^{s}|\,dx+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(\hat{x}}|v/d^{s}|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq M_{\rho/16}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}-v/d^{s}|)(\hat{x})+\|v/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{\rho/4}(z))}\leq c\lambda.

(b)

If
r\in[\rho/16,2^{-10}]
, then we first observe
B_{r}(\hat{x})\subset B_{8r}(\widetilde{x})
.
Therefore, using this and (
4.19
), we have that there is a constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
satisfying

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(\hat{x})}|u/d^{s}|\,dx\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{8r}(\widetilde{x})}|u/d^{s}|\,dx\leq c\lambda.

(c)

If
r\in[2^{-10},2^{-6}]
, then we get

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(\hat{x})}|u/d^{s}|\,dx\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{1/2}}|u/d^{s}|\,dx\leq c\lambda

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.

From
(a)
,
(b)
and
(c)
, selecting
N
large enough depending only on
c
as appearing above, we get that
M_{2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}|)(\hat{x})\leq N\lambda
, which implies
\hat{x}\notin\mathcal{C}\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)
. This proves (
4.26
). Therefore, by (
4.26
), the weak
(1,1)
-estimate and (
4.25
), we get

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)|

\displaystyle\leq|\{x\in\Omega\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)\,:\,M_{\rho/16}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}-v/d^{s}|)(x)>\lambda\}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\lambda}|B_{\rho}|\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{\rho/4}(z)}|u/d^{s}-v/d^{s}|\,dx\leq c\varepsilon_{1}|B_{\rho}|

which contradicts the assumption of the lemma for
\varepsilon=c\varepsilon_{1}
. This completes the proof.
∎

We next observe the Vitali type covering lemma (see
[
BW04
, Lemma 2.7]
).

Lemma 4.5
.

Let
\varepsilon>0
and
\mathcal{C}\subset\mathcal{D}\subset\Omega\cap B_{1/4}
. Assume that

|\mathcal{C}|\leq\varepsilon

and that for any
z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}
and
\rho\in(0,2^{-9}]
,

B_{\rho/8}(z)\cap\Omega\subset\mathcal{D}

holds whenever
|\mathcal{C}\cap B_{\rho/8}(z)|\geq\varepsilon|B_{\rho/8}|
. Then there is a constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
such that

|\mathcal{C}|\leq c\varepsilon|\mathcal{D}|.

From (
4.18
), Lemma
4.4
and Lemma
4.5
together with the usage of an inductive argument, we can obtain the following.

Corollary 4.6
.

For
\varepsilon>0
, there is a constant
\delta=\delta(\mathsf{data},\varepsilon)
such that if
A
is
(\delta,1/2)
-vanishing, then for all
\lambda\geq\lambda_{1}
and non-negative integer
k
, there holds

\displaystyle\big|\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}|)(z)>N^{k}\lambda\}\big|

\displaystyle\leq(c\varepsilon)^{k}\big|\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}|)(z)>\lambda\}\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k}(c\varepsilon)^{i}\big|\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2_{*}s,2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z)>(N^{k-i}\delta\lambda)^{2_{*}}\}\big|,

for some
c=c(\mathsf{data})
, where
N=N(\mathsf{data})\geq 1
is given by Lemma
4.4
.

Using this, we are able to prove the following lemma.

Lemma 4.7
.

Let
f\in L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1/2})
for some
q\in(2_{*},n/s)
. Then there is a constant
\delta=\delta(\mathsf{data},q)
such that if
A
is
(\delta,1/2)
-vanishing, then

\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}}(\Omega\cap B_{1/4})}\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1/2})}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},q)
.

Proof.

Let us fix
N=N(\mathsf{data})\geq 1
, which is determined in Lemma
4.4
. Then for any
\varepsilon>0
, there is a constant
\delta=\delta(\mathsf{data},\varepsilon)
such that (
4.6
) with
\lambda=\lambda_{1}
holds, where the constant
\lambda_{1}
is determined in (
4.17
). There is a constant
\varepsilon_{1}=\varepsilon_{1}(\mathsf{data})\in(0,1)
such that if
\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{1}
, then by taking summation over
k
on both sides of (
4.6
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{l}(N^{k}\lambda_{1})^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}}\big|\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}|)(z)>N^{k}\lambda_{1}\}\big|

(4.27)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{l}\sum_{i=1}^{k}(N^{k-1}\lambda_{1})^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}}(c\varepsilon)^{i}\big|\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2_{*}s,2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z)>(N^{k-i}\delta\lambda_{1})^{2_{*}}\}\big|\eqqcolon I,

where
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. Moreover, we further estimate the right-hand side of (
4.27
) as

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{l}(c\varepsilon N^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}})^{i}\sum_{k=i}^{l}\left(N^{k-i}\lambda_{1}\right)^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}}\big|\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2_{*}s,2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z)>(N^{k-i}\delta\lambda_{1})^{2_{*}}\}\big|

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}\delta^{-\frac{nq}{n-sq}}\sum_{i=1}^{l}(c\varepsilon N^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}})^{i}\left(\|M_{2_{*}s,2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z)\|^{2_{*}}_{L^{\frac{nq}{2_{*}(n-sq)}}(\Omega\cap B_{1/4})}+\lambda_{1}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
and
c_{0}=c_{0}(\mathsf{data},q)
, where we have also used
[
CC95
, Lemma 7.3]
. Thus we now take
\varepsilon_{2}=\varepsilon_{2}(\mathsf{data},q)
such that if
\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{2}
, then

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{l}(N^{k}\lambda_{1})^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}}\big|\{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/4}\,:\,M_{2^{-6}}^{\Omega}(|u/d^{s}|)(z)>N^{k}\lambda_{1}\}\big|\leq c\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1/2})}+c\lambda_{1},

where
c=c(\mathsf{data},q)
. We now fix
\varepsilon=\min\{\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2}\}
which depends only on
\mathsf{data}
and
q
. Then the constant
\delta=\delta(\mathsf{data},q)
is now chosen by Lemma
4.4
. By
[
CC95
, Lemma 7.3]
and (
4.17
), we get

(4.28)

\displaystyle\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}}(\Omega\cap B_{1/4})}\leq c\left(\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{1}(\Omega\cap B_{1/2})}+\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1/2})}\right),

where we have used

\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{1/2})\leq c\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}.

We are going to prove for any
z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/2}
,

(4.29)

\displaystyle\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{1}(\Omega\cap B_{2^{-6}}(z))}\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{2_{*}}(\Omega\cap B_{2^{-3}}(z))}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Let
w
be a weak solution to (
3.2
) with
r=2^{-4}
and let
v
be a weak solution to (
3.5
) with
r=2^{-4}
. Then we obtain

(4.30)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{-6}}(z)}|{u}/d^{s}|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-6}}(z)}|({u}-v)/d^{s}|\,dx+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-6}}(z)}|v/d^{s}|\,dx\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\left([{u}-v]_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+(\|v\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-4}}(z))}+\mathrm{Tail}({u};B_{2^{-4}}(z)))\right),

where we have used Lemma
4.2
. We now use Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
to see that

(4.31)

\displaystyle{}_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}

\displaystyle\leq[{u}-w]_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+[w-v]_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{-4}}(z)}|f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+\|w\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}+\mathrm{Tail}(w;B_{2^{-3}}(z))\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. In addition, by an aid of Lemma
3.1
, we have

(4.32)

\displaystyle\|w\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}+\mathrm{Tail}(w;B_{2^{-3}}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{-3}}(z)}|f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+\|u\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}+\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{2^{-3}}(z))\right].

Combining (
4.31
) and (
4.32
) yields

(4.33)

\displaystyle[u-v]_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}\leq c\left[\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{-3}}(z)}|f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+\|{u}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}+\mathrm{Tail}({u};B_{2^{-3}}(z))\right]

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. We now employ Sobolev embedding as in
[
Coz17
, Lemma 4.8]
along with (
4.31
) and (
4.32
) to see that

\displaystyle\|v\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq\|v-w\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}+\|w-u\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}+\|{u}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq c\left([{v}-w]_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+[w-u]_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|{u}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{-3}}(z)}|f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+\|{u}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}+\mathrm{Tail}({u};B_{2^{-3}}(z))\right].

Plugging this and (
4.33
) into (
4.30
) together with (
5.52
) gives

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-6}}(z)}|{u}/d^{s}|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{-3}}(z)}|f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+\|{u}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-3}}(z))}+\mathrm{Tail}({u};B_{2^{-3}}(z))\right]

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
, which implies (
4.29
). Therefore, using a standard covering argument, we have

(4.34)

\displaystyle\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{1}(\Omega\cap B_{1/2})}\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{2_{*}}(\Omega\cap B_{1/2})}\right).

Plugging this into (
4.28
) yields the desired estimate.
∎

4.2.
Proof of Theorem
1.4

We are now in a position to present the proof of Theorem
1.4
.

Proof of Theorem
1.4
.

By the scaling invariant property, we may assume
\rho_{0}=1
. We now fix
\delta=\delta(\mathsf{data},q)
as given by Lemma
4.7
. In addition, by Remark
4.1
, we have that there is a weak solution
\widetilde{u}
to (
4.1
) with
(\delta,1)
-vanishing coefficient
A
. By Lemma
4.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|{u}/d^{s}\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}}(\Omega\cap B_{1/4})}=\|\widetilde{u}/d^{s}\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}}(\Omega\cap B_{1/4})}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|\widetilde{u}\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+c\|f+g_{2}\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|{u}\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},q)
, where we have also used (
4.2
) and the fact that
\widetilde{u}\equiv u
on
B_{3/4}
and
|\widetilde{u}(x)|\leq|u(x)|
for any
x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}
. This completes the proof.
∎

5.
The case of continuous coefficients

In this section, we consider a weak solution
u\in W^{s,2}(B_{1})\cap L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})
to (
1.5
), where the associated kernel coefficient
A
is continuous in
B_{1}\times B_{1}
and there is a non-decreasing function
\omega:{\mathbb{R}}_{+}\to{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
such that

|A(x_{0},y_{0})-A(x_{1},y_{1})|\leq\omega(\max\{|x_{0}-x_{1}|,|y_{0}-y_{1}|\})\quad\text{for any }x_{0},x_{1},y_{0},y_{1}\in B_{1}.

We now fix
z\in\overline{\Omega}\cap B_{1/2}
. Then there is a function
\phi\equiv\phi_{z}
which is the solution to

(5.1)

\left\{\begin{aligned} \mathcal{L}_{A_{z}}{\phi}&=0&&\qquad\mbox{in $\Omega\cap B_{1}$},\\
\phi&=g&&\qquad\mbox{in ${\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus(\Omega\cap B_{1})$},\end{aligned}\right.

where
A_{z}\coloneqq A(z,z)
and
g\in C_{c}^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus{B}_{3/2})
with
g\equiv 1
on
B_{2}\setminus B_{7/4}
and
0\leq g\leq 1
.
By
[
FR24
, Lemma 2.3.9, Proposition 2.6.4, Proposition 2.6.6]
, the maximum principle given in
[
FR24
, Lemma 2.3.3]
and
[
FR24
, Proposition 2.7.8]
, we observe

(5.2)

\displaystyle{d^{s}(x)}/{c}\leq\phi(x)\leq cd^{s}(x)\quad\text{if }x\in B_{3/4},

(5.3)

\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}\leq c

and

(5.4)

\displaystyle\|\phi/d^{s}\|_{C^{\alpha}(\Omega\cap B_{3/4})}\leq c,

where
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
By Lemma
2.3
, we have the following remark, which enables us to consider a localized problem instead, where the associated kernel coefficient is constant when it is sufficiently far from the given domain.

Remark 5.1
.

We observe that
u\in W^{s,2}(B_{1/8}(z))\cap L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})
is a weak solution to

\left\{\begin{aligned} \mathcal{L}_{A}u&=f&&\qquad\mbox{in $\Omega\cap B_{1/8}(z)$},\\
u&=0&&\qquad\mbox{in $B_{1/8}(z)\setminus\Omega$}.\end{aligned}\right.

By Lemma
2.3
with
R=1/8
,
x_{0}=z
and
a=A(z,z)
, there is a weak solution
\widetilde{u}\in W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})
to

(5.5)

\left\{\begin{aligned} \mathcal{L}_{\widetilde{A}}\widetilde{u}&=F&&\qquad\mbox{in $\Omega\cap B_{1/16}(z)$},\\
\widetilde{u}&=0&&\qquad\mbox{in $B_{1/16}(z)\setminus\Omega$},\\
\widetilde{u}&=u&&\qquad\mbox{in $B_{5/64}(z)$},\end{aligned}\right.

where
\widetilde{u}\equiv 0
in
{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{1/8}(z)
and

(5.6)

|F(x)|\leq|f(x)|+c\left(|u(x)|+\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{5/64}(z))\right)\quad\text{for any }x\in B_{1/16}(z),

and some
c=c(n,s,\Lambda)
. In addition, there is a constant
c=c(n,s,\Lambda)
such that

|\widetilde{A}(x,y)-\widetilde{A}(z,z)|\leq{\widetilde{\omega}}(\max\{|x-z|,|y-z|\})\quad\text{for any }x,y\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n},

where

\displaystyle{\widetilde{\omega}}(\rho)=\begin{cases}\omega(\rho)\quad&\text{if }\rho\leq 1/8,\\
0&\text{if }\rho>1/8.\end{cases}

5.1.
Localized problems

Throughout this subsection, in light of the Remark
5.1
, we consider a weak solution
u\in W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})
to

(5.7)

\left\{\begin{aligned} \mathcal{L}_{A}u&=f&&\qquad\mbox{in $\Omega\cap B_{1/16}(z)$},\\
u&=0&&\qquad\mbox{in $B_{1/8}(z)\setminus\Omega$}.\end{aligned}\right.

In addition, the kernel coefficient
A
satisfies

(5.8)

|{A}(x,y)-{A}(z,z)|\leq{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\max\{|x-z|,|y-z|\})\quad\text{for any }x,y\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n},

where

(5.9)

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)=\begin{cases}\omega(\rho)\quad&\text{if }\rho\leq 1/8,\\
0&\text{if }\rho>1/8\end{cases}

for some non-decreasing function
\omega:{\mathbb{R}}^{+}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{+}
.
To get a control on the global behavior of the oscillation of the kernel coefficient, we define a function
{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}:{\mathbb{R}}_{+}\to{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
as

{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(\rho)\coloneqq\rho^{s}\int_{\rho}^{\infty}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\xi)}{\xi^{1+s}}\,d\xi\quad\text{for all }\rho>0.

We now define the following excess functionals

E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{\rho}(z))\coloneqq\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{\rho}(z)}|u/\phi-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho}(z)}|\,dx

and

\displaystyle{E}(u;B_{\rho}(z))

\displaystyle\coloneqq E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{\rho}(z))+\max\{\phi(z),\rho^{s}\}^{-1}\mathrm{Tail}(u-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho}(z)}\phi;B_{\rho}(z)).

We point out that such excess functionals were first introduced in
[
KW24a
]
.
We then observe the following inequality.

Lemma 5.2
.

There is a constant
c=c(n,s)
such that for any
0<\rho<r\leq 1/16
,

{E}(u;B_{r}(z))\leq c{(r/\rho)^{n}}{E}(u;B_{r}(z)).

Proof.

We first observe that

\displaystyle\max\{\phi(z),\rho^{s}\}^{-1}\mathrm{Tail}(u-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho}(z)}\phi;B_{\rho}(z))

\displaystyle\leq\max\{\phi(z),\rho^{s}\}^{-1}\rho^{2s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{\rho}(z)}\frac{|u-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}\phi|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+\max\{\phi(z),\rho^{s}\}^{-1}\rho^{2s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{\rho}(z)}\frac{|((u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho}(z)})\phi|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\coloneqq I+J.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq\max\{\phi(z),\rho^{s}\}^{-1}\left[\rho^{2s}\int_{B_{r}(z)\setminus B_{\rho}(z)}\frac{|u-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}\phi|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy+\rho^{2s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{r}(z)}\frac{|u-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}\phi|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\right].

We note from (
5.2
) that

(5.10)

|\phi(y)|\leq c|d(y)|^{s}\leq c(d(z)+2^{k}r)^{s}\quad\text{for any }y\in B_{2^{k}r}(z),

for all non-negative integer
k
such that
B_{2^{k}r}(z)\subset B_{3/4}
. In addition, there is a nonnegative integer
j
such that
2^{j}\rho<r<2^{j+1}\rho
.
Thus, we get

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\sum_{i=0}^{j-1}2^{-is}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{i}\rho}(z)}|u/\phi-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|\,dy+E(u/B_{r}(z))\right)\leq c(r/\rho)^{n}E(u;B_{r}(z))

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. Let us denote
l
as a positive integer such that
1/8<2^{l}\rho\leq 1/4
.
We now estimate
J
as

\displaystyle J

\displaystyle\leq\max\{\phi(z),\rho^{s}\}^{-1}\rho^{2s}\sum_{i=0}^{l-1}\int_{B_{2^{i+1}\rho}(z)\setminus B_{2^{i}r}(z)}\frac{|((u/\phi)_{B_{\rho}(z)}-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)})\phi|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+\max\{\phi(z),\rho^{s}\}^{-1}\rho^{2s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2^{l}\rho}(z)}\frac{|((u/\phi)_{B_{\rho}(z)}-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)})\phi|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{\phi(z),\rho^{s}\}^{-1}\rho^{2s}\sum_{i=0}^{l-1}(2^{i}\rho)^{-2s}\max\{d(z),(2^{i}\rho)\}^{s}|(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho}(z)}-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|

\displaystyle\quad+c|(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho}(z)}-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|

\displaystyle\leq c|(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho}(z)}-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
, where we have used (
5.10
) with
r
replaced by
\rho
and (
5.3
). Thus, we get

\displaystyle J\leq c(r/\rho)^{n}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{r}(z)).

Combining the estimates of
I
and
J
together with

\displaystyle E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{\rho}(z))\leq c(r/\rho)^{n}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{r}(z)),

we have the desired estimate.
∎

In light of the comparison estimates given in Section
3
and
[
KW24a
, Lemma 6.1]
, we now prove the following.

Lemma 5.3
.

Let
r\in(0,1/64]
and let
v
be the weak solution to (
3.5
) with
a_{0}\equiv A_{z}=A(z,z)
. Then for any
\rho\in(0,1/4]
,

(5.11)

{E}_{\mathrm{loc}}(v;B_{\rho r}(z))\leq c\rho^{\alpha}{E}(v;B_{r}(z))

holds for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. In addition, we have

(5.12)

\displaystyle{E}_{\mathrm{loc}}(u-v;B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2r))({E}(u;B_{2r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|)

\displaystyle+c\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|\cdot-z|);B_{2r}(z))

\displaystyle+c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}.

Proof.

We first note that (
5.11
) follows from
[
KW24a
, Lemma 6.6]
and (
2.1
).
We are now going to prove (
5.12
). Let
w
be the weak solution to (
3.2
).
By Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
, we get

(5.13)

\displaystyle r^{-n}[u-v]^{2}_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{2}{2_{*}}}

\displaystyle+c({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2r))^{2}(r^{-2n}\|w/r^{s}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}+r^{-2s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{2r}(z))^{2})

\displaystyle+cr^{2s}\left(\sup_{x\in B_{r}(z)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{|w(y)||A-A(z,z)|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\right)^{2}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
, where we have also used the following simple observation

\displaystyle r^{4s}\left(\sup_{x\in B_{r}(z)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{|A-A_{z}|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\right)^{2}\leq r^{4s}\left(\int_{2r}^{\infty}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho^{1+2s}}\,d\rho\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq r^{2s}\left(\int_{2r}^{\infty}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho^{1+s}}\,d\rho\right)^{2}.

Moreover, by Hölder’s inequality, Sobolev’s inequality and Lemma
3.1
, we have

\displaystyle r^{-2n}\|w/r^{s}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq r^{-2n}\|(w-u)/r^{s}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}+r^{-2n}\|u/r^{s}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq cr^{-n}[w-u]^{2}_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+r^{-2n}\|u/r^{s}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{2}{2_{*}}}+r^{-2n}\|u/r^{s}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}\right],

where
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. Additionally, by (
4.7
) and (
5.2
), we get

\displaystyle r^{-n}\|u/r^{s}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{1,d(z)/r\}^{s}r^{-n}\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{1,d(z)/r\}^{s}r^{-n}\|u/\phi^{s}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{1,d(z)/r\}^{s}(E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
, which gives

\displaystyle r^{-2n}\|w/r^{s}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(B_{2r}(z))}

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{1,d(z)/r\}^{s}(E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|)

\displaystyle\quad+c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{2}{2_{*}}}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Plugging this into (
5.13
) yields

(5.14)

\displaystyle r^{-n}[u-v]^{2}_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{2}{2_{*}}}

\displaystyle+c({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2r))^{2}\max\{1,d(z)/r\}^{2s}(E(u;B_{2r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|)^{2}

\displaystyle+c({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2r))^{2}r^{-2s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{2r}(z))^{2}

\displaystyle+cr^{-2s}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|\cdot-z|);B_{2r}(z))^{2}.

To further simplify the third term on the right-hand side of the above expression, we observe that

\displaystyle r^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{2r}(z))\leq r^{-s}\mathrm{Tail}(u-(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}\phi;B_{2r}(z))+r^{s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}\phi(y)|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{1,d(z)/r\}^{s}E(u;B_{2r}(z))+c\underbrace{\sum_{k=0}^{l}(2^{2k}r)^{-s}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{k}r}(z)}|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}\phi|\,dy}_{{=:I_{1}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{r^{s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2^{l}r}(z)}\frac{|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}\phi(y)|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy}_{\eqqcolon I_{2}}

for some constant
c=c(n,s)
, where the positive integer
l
satisfies
1/32\leq 2^{l}r<1/16
and
B_{2^{l}r}(z)\subset B_{3/8}
.
Using (
5.10
) and (
5.2
), we estimate
I_{1}
as

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq c\sum_{k=0}^{l}(2^{2k}r)^{-s}|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|(d(z)+2^{k}r)^{s}

\displaystyle\leq c(d^{s}(z)/r^{s}+1)|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{1,d(z)/r\}^{s}|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. We next estimate
I_{2}
as

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq cr^{s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2^{l}r}(z)}\frac{|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\leq c|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|,

where we have used (
5.3
) and the fact that
B_{1/8}(z)\subset B_{2^{l}\rho}(z)
. Plugging the estimates
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
into (
5.14
) gives us

(5.15)

\displaystyle r^{-n}[u-v]^{2}_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{2}{2_{*}}}

\displaystyle+c({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2r))^{2}\max\{1,d(z)/r\}^{2s}(E(u;B_{2r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2r}(z)}|)^{2}

\displaystyle+cr^{-2s}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|\cdot-z|);B_{2r}(z))^{2}.

To estimate the left-hand side of (
5.15
),
we use (
5.2
) and Lemma
2.5
to get

\displaystyle\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(z)}|u/\phi-v/\phi|\,dx\right)^{2}\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(z)}|u/d^{s}-v/d^{s}|^{2}\leq cr^{-n}\frac{1}{\max\{1,d(z)/r\}^{2s}}[u-v]_{W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}^{2}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Combining this and (
5.15
) together with (
5.2
), we have (
5.12
).
∎

We now prove the following decay estimates.

Lemma 5.4
.

Let
r\in(0,1/64]
. Then, for any
\rho\in(0,1/4]
, we have

\displaystyle{E}(u;B_{\rho r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\rho^{\alpha}{E}(u;B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\quad+c\rho^{-n}({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(r))({E}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|)

\displaystyle\quad+c\rho^{-n}\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|\cdot-z|);B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\quad+c\rho^{-n}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}},

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.

Proof.

If
\rho\in[2^{-10},2^{-3}]
, then the desired estimate follows directly from Lemma
5.2
. Thus, we now assume
\rho\in(0,2^{-10})
. Then there is a positive integer
N_{\rho}
such that

(5.16)

2^{-7}\leq 2^{N_{\rho}}\rho<2^{-5}.

We note that

\displaystyle{E}_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{\rho r}(z))\leq{E}_{\mathrm{loc}}(v;B_{\rho r}(z))+{E}_{\mathrm{loc}}(u-v;B_{\rho r}(z))\eqqcolon I_{1}+I_{2},

where
v
is the weak solution to (
3.2
) with
a_{0}
and
r
replaced by
A(z,z)
and
r/2
, respectively.
In light of (
5.11
) in Lemma
5.3
with
r
replaced by
r/2
, we first estimate
I_{1}
as

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq c\rho^{\alpha}{E}(v;B_{r/2}(z))\leq c\rho^{\alpha}{E}(u;B_{r/2}(z))+c\rho^{\alpha}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(v-u;B_{r/2}(z))

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
By (
5.12
) in Lemma
5.3
with
r
replaced by
r/2
, we next estimate
I_{2}
as

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq c\rho^{-n}({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(r))({E}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|)

\displaystyle\quad+c\rho^{-n}{\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|\cdot-z|);B_{r}(z))}+c\rho^{-n}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}},

where
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Combining all the estimates
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
yields

(5.17)

\displaystyle{E}_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{\rho r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\rho^{\alpha}{E}(u;B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle+c\rho^{-n}({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(r))({E}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|)

\displaystyle+c\rho^{-n}\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|\cdot-z|);B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle+c\rho^{-n}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\eqqcolon I,

where
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. Using
[
DKLN24a
, Lemma 2.2]
, we observe that

(5.18)

\displaystyle\max\{\phi(z),(\rho r)^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot)-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho r}(z)}\phi;B_{\rho r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{\phi(z),(\rho r)^{s}\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-2sk}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{k}\rho r}(z)}|u-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho r}(z)}\phi|\,dx

\displaystyle+c\max\{\phi(z),(\rho r)^{s}\}^{-1}(\rho r)^{2s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2^{N_{\rho}}\rho r}(z)}\frac{|u-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho r}(z)}\phi|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\eqqcolon J_{1}+J_{2}.

We next note from (
5.10
) with
r
and
z
replaced by
\rho r
and
0
, respectively, and (
5.2
) that

(5.19)

|\phi(y)|\leq c(\phi(z)+(2^{k}\rho r)^{s})\leq c\max\{\phi(z),(2^{k}\rho r)^{s}\}\quad\text{for any }y\in B_{2^{k}\rho r}(z)\text{ with }k=0,1,\cdots,N_{\rho}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Using this, we now estimate
J_{1}
as

\displaystyle J_{1}\leq c\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-ks}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{k}\rho r}(z)}|u/\phi-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho r}(z)}|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-ks}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{j}\rho r}(z)}|u/\phi-(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{j}\rho r}(z)}|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{j=1}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-js}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{j}\rho r}(z)}|u/\phi-(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{j}\rho r}(z)}|\,dx,

where we have also used Fubini’s theorem and the fact that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=j}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-ks}\leq c2^{-js}.

After a few simple calculations along with (
5.16
), (
5.19
) and the fact that
\alpha<s
, we get

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\rho^{2s}\max\{\phi(z),(\rho r)^{s}\}^{-1}r^{2s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2^{N_{\rho}}\rho r}(z)}\frac{|u-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho r}(z)}\phi|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-N_{\rho}s}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{k}\rho r}(z)}|u/\phi-(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{k}\rho r}(z)}|\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+c\left[\rho^{s}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}(z)}|u/\phi-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|\,dx+\rho^{s}\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}\mathrm{Tail}(u-(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}\phi;B_{r}(z))\right]

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-ks}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{k}\rho r}(z)}|u/\phi-(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{k}\rho r}(z)}|\,dx+\rho^{s}{E}(u;B_{r}(z))\right]

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Combining all the estimates
J_{1}
and
J_{2}
, we have

(5.20)

\displaystyle J_{1}+J_{2}

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-ks}{E}_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{k}\rho r}(z))+\rho^{s}{E}(u;B_{r}(z))\right].

We now use (
5.17
) and the fact that
\alpha<s
to find

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}2^{-ks}{E}_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{k}\rho r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}c2^{-ks}(2^{k}\rho)^{\alpha}{E}(u;B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}c2^{-ks}(2^{k}\rho)^{-n}({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(r))({E}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}c2^{-ks}(2^{k}\rho)^{-n}\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|\cdot-z|);B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=1}^{N_{\rho}}c2^{-ks}(2^{k}\rho)^{-n}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(z)}(r^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq cI,

where
I
is as determined in (
5.17
). By plugging this into (
5.20
) and taking into account (
5.18
), we get

\displaystyle\max\{\phi(z),(\rho r)^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}((u(\cdot)-(u/\phi)_{B_{\rho r}(z)}\phi;B_{\rho r}(z))\leq cI+c\rho^{s}{E}(u;B_{r}(z))

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. This coupled with (
5.17
) gives the desired estimate.
∎

We are now able to get a uniform control on the averages of
u/\phi
on small balls by the Wolff-potential of
|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}
.

Lemma 5.5
.

Let us assume
A
satisfies

(5.21)

\int_{0}^{1/8}\frac{{{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho}\,d\rho\leq N

for some constant
N>0
, where
\omega
is the non-decreasing function given in (
5.9
). Then there is a constant
m=m(\mathsf{data})\geq 1
such that for any
r\in(0,1/64]
and
i\geq 1
, we have

(5.22)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-im}r}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx\leq c\left({E}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|+{\mathrm{Tail}}(u/r^{s};B_{r}(z))+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,r)\right),

where
c=c(\mathsf{data},N,{{\omega}})
.

Proof.

We first fix
m\geq 1
and
R_{0}\in(0,1/32]
which are determined later in (
5.23
) and (
5.33
), respectively. We then select
r\in(0,R_{0}]
. By applying Lemma
5.4
with
r
and
\rho
replaced by
2^{-km}r
and
2^{-m}
, respectively, to each
k\in\{0,\dots,l\}
, for any
l\geq 1
fixed, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{l}E(u;B_{2^{-m(k+1)}r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c2^{-m\alpha}\sum_{k=0}^{l}E(u;B_{2^{-mk}r}(z))

\displaystyle\quad+c2^{-mn}\sum_{k=0}^{l}({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{-mk}r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2^{-mk}r))({E}(u;B_{2^{-mk}r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{-mk}r}(z)}|)

\displaystyle\quad+c2^{-mn}\sum_{k=0}^{l}\max\{\phi(z),(2^{-mk}r)^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|\cdot-z|);B_{2^{-mk}r}(z))

\displaystyle\quad+c2^{-mn}\sum_{k=0}^{l}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{-mk}r}(z)}((2^{-mk}r)^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\eqqcolon\sum_{i-1}^{4}I_{i}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
We first choose
m=m(\mathsf{data})\geq 1
so that

(5.23)

c2^{-m\alpha}\leq 1/4.

Thus we have

(5.24)

I_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{l}E(u;B_{2^{-mk}r}(z))+E(u;B_{r}(z))\right].

Using (
5.21
) and (
5.9
), we observe

(5.25)

d(R)\coloneqq\int_{0}^{R}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho}\,d\rho=\int_{0}^{\min\{R,1/8\}}\frac{{{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho}\,d\rho\leq N\quad\text{for any }R>0.

Moreover, we also get that for any
\varepsilon>0
, there are
r_{0}\in(0,1/8]
and
k_{0}\geq 2
such that

(5.26)

d(r_{0})\leq\varepsilon\quad\text{and}\quad 2^{-sk_{0}m}\leq\varepsilon,

where
r_{0}
and
k_{0}
depend on
\mathsf{data}
and
\varepsilon
.
We now observe from (
5.9
) that

\displaystyle\int_{2^{km}R}^{2^{(k+1)m}R}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho^{1+s}}\,d\rho\leq\int_{2^{km}R}^{2^{(k+1)m}R}\frac{{{\omega}}(2^{(k+1)m}R)}{(2^{km}R)^{1+s}}\,d\rho\leq c\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(k+1)m}R)}{(2^{(k+1)m}R)^{s}}\quad\text{if }2^{{k}m}R\leq 2^{-3-2m},

\displaystyle\int_{2^{km}R}^{2^{(k+1)m}R}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho^{1+s}}\,d\rho\leq\int_{2^{-2m-3}}^{2^{-3}}\frac{{{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho^{1+s}}\,d\rho\quad\text{if }2^{-3+m}<2^{{k}m}R\leq 2^{-3}

and

(5.27)

\displaystyle\int_{2^{km}R}^{2^{(k+1)m}R}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho^{1+s}}\,d\rho=0=\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(k+1)m}R)}{(2^{(k+1)m}R)^{s}}\quad\text{if }2^{km}R>2^{-3}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Using these observations, we have for any
R\leq R_{0}\coloneqq 2^{-k_{0}m}r_{0}
and
l\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{l}{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2^{-im}R

\displaystyle)\leq\sum_{i=0}^{l}(2^{-im}R)^{s}\int_{2^{-im}R}^{\infty}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho^{1+s}}\,d\rho

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i=0}^{l}(2^{-im}R)^{s}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{k_{0}-1}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-i+j)m}R)}{(2^{(-i+j)m}R)^{s}}+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-i+k_{0}+j)m}R)}{(2^{(-i+k_{0}+j)m}R)^{s}}+\int^{2^{-3}}_{2^{-2m-3}}\frac{\omega(\rho)}{\rho^{1+s}}\,d\rho\right]

\displaystyle\eqqcolon J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})

We note that in the above estimates, the constant
c
depends on
m
but by virtue of (
5.23
), eventually it depends only on
\mathsf{data}
.
By Fubini’s theorem, (
5.26
), we have

\displaystyle J_{1}\leq\sum_{j=0}^{k_{0}-1}\sum_{i=0}^{l}{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-i+j)m}R)2^{-sjm}

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{j=0}^{k_{0}-1}2^{-sjm}\sum_{i=0}^{l}\int_{2^{(-i+j)m}R}^{{2^{(-i+j+1)m}R}}\frac{{{\omega}}(2^{(-i+j)m}R)}{\rho}\,d\rho

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{j=0}^{k_{0}-1}2^{-sjm}\sum_{i=0}^{l}\int_{2^{(-i+j)m}R}^{{2^{(-i+j+1)m}R}}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho}\,d\rho

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{j=0}^{k_{0}-1}d(2^{(j+1)m}R)2^{-sjm}\leq cd(r_{0})\sum_{j=0}^{k_{0}-1}2^{-sjm}\leq c\varepsilon,

where
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Similarly, we employ Fubini’s theorem, (
5.27
) and (
5.25
) to see that

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i=0}^{l}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-i+k_{0}+j)m}R)}{2^{s(k_{0}+j)m}}

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}2^{-s(k_{0}+j)m}\left[\sum_{i=0}^{l}\int_{2^{(-i+j+k_{0})m}R}^{{2^{(-i+j+1+k_{0})m}R}}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho}\,d\rho+2^{-s(k_{0}+j)m}\|\omega\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{1/8)}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}2^{-s(k_{0}+j)m}d(2^{(k_{0}+j+1)m}R)\leq c2^{-sk_{0}m}\leq c\varepsilon

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},{{\omega}},N)
. After a few simple computations, we estimate
J_{3}
as

J_{3}\leq c\sum_{i=0}^{l}(2^{-im}R)^{s}N\leq cR_{0}^{s}\leq c2^{-sk_{0}m}\leq c\varepsilon

for some
c=c(\mathsf{data},N)
, where we have used (
5.25
) and (
5.26
).
On the other hand, we have

\sum_{i=0}^{l}{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{-im}R)\leq c\sum_{i=0}^{l}\int_{2^{-im}R}^{2^{(-i+1)m}R}\frac{{{\omega}}(\rho)}{\rho}\,d\rho\leq cd(r_{0})\leq c\varepsilon.

Using this together with the estimates
J_{1}
,
J_{2}
and
J_{3}
, we have if
R\leq R_{0}

(5.28)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{l}({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{-im}R)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2^{-im}R))\leq c\varepsilon

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},{{\omega}},N)
.

We therefore estimate
I_{2}
as

(5.29)

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq c2^{-mn}\left[\sum_{k=0}^{l}({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{-mk}r)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2^{-mk}r))\right]\left[\sum_{i=1}^{l}{E}(u;B_{2^{-mi}r}(z))+E(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|\right]

\displaystyle\leq c\varepsilon\left(\sum_{k=0}^{l}{E}(u;B_{2^{-mk}r}(z))+E(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
provided that
r\leq R_{0}
, where we have used

(5.30)

\displaystyle|(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{-km}r}}(z)|

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i=1}^{k}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{-im}r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i=1}^{l}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{-im}r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|

and (
5.28
). To estimate
I_{3}
, we first note from
[
DKLN24a
, Lemma 2.2]
that

\displaystyle\max\{\phi(z),(2^{-mk}r)^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\cdot-z);B_{2^{-mk}r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\max\{\phi(z),(2^{-mk}r)^{s}\}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}2^{-2s(im)}{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-k+i)m}r)\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{(-k+i)m}r}(z)}|u|\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+c\max\{\phi(z),(2^{-mk}r)^{s}\}^{-1}2^{-2skm}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\cdot-z);B_{r}(z))\coloneqq I_{3,1}(k)+I_{3,2}(k).

By means of (
5.19
) with
\rho=1
, we estimate
I_{3,1}(k)
as

\displaystyle I_{3,1}(k)

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i=0}^{k}2^{-s(im)}{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-k+i)m}r)\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{(-k+i)m}r}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dy

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i=0}^{k}2^{-s(im)}{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-k+i)m}r)(E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{(-k+i)m}r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{(-k+i)m}r}(z)}|).

In addition, we estimate
I_{3,2}(k)
as

\displaystyle I_{3,2}(k)\leq c\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}2^{-skm}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\cdot-z);B_{r}(z)).

By combining the estimates
I_{3,1}(k)
and
I_{3,2}(k)
along with Fubini’s theorem, we have

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{k=0}^{l}I_{3,1}(k)+I_{3,2}(k)

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{k=0}^{l}\sum_{i=0}^{k}2^{-s(im)}{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-k+i)m}r)(E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{(-k+i)m}r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{(-k+i)m}r}(z)}|)

\displaystyle\quad+c\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}|\cdot|;B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i=0}^{l}\sum_{k=i}^{l}2^{-s(im)}{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-k+i)m}r)(E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{(-k+i)m}r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{(-k+i)m}r}(z)}|)

\displaystyle\quad+c\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\cdot-z);B_{r}(z))

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
We then employ (
5.28
) to further estimate
I_{3}
as

(5.31)

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i=0}^{l}2^{-s(im)}\left[\sum_{k=i}^{l}{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(2^{(-k+i)m}r)\right]\left[\sum_{j=0}^{l}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{-jm}r}(z))+E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|\right]

\displaystyle+c\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\cdot-z);B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\varepsilon\sum_{i=0}^{l}2^{-s(im)}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{l}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{-jm}r}(z))+E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|\right]

\displaystyle+c\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\cdot-z);B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\varepsilon\left[\sum_{j=0}^{l}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{-jm}r}(z))+E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|\right]

\displaystyle+c\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\cdot-z);B_{r}(z)),

where
c=c(\mathsf{data},{{\omega}},N)
whenever
r\leq R_{0}
.

We now estimate
I_{4}
as

(5.32)

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{k=0}^{l}\int_{2^{-m(k+1)}r}^{2^{-mk}r}\left(\frac{\||f|^{2_{*}}\chi_{\Omega}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-mk}r})(z)}}{(2^{-mk}r)^{n-2_{*}s}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\frac{\,d\rho}{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{k=0}^{l}\int_{2^{-m(k+1)}r}^{2^{-mk}r}\left(\frac{\||f|^{2_{*}}\chi_{\Omega}\|_{L^{1}(B_{\rho}(z))}}{\rho^{n-2_{*}s}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\frac{\,d\rho}{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq c\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\chi_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,r),

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
, where we have used (
2.1
).
Combining all the estimates (
5.24
), (
5.29
), (
5.31
) and (
5.32
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{l}E(u;B_{2^{-m(k+1)}r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\sum_{k=1}^{l}E(u;B_{2^{-mk}r}(z))+cE(u;B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\quad+c\varepsilon\left(\sum_{k=1}^{l}{E}(u;B_{2^{-mk}r}(z))+E(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|\right)

\displaystyle\quad+c\max\{\phi(z),r^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\cdot-z);B_{r}(z))+c\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,r)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},{\omega},N)
, whenever
r\leq R_{0}
.
We now take
r_{0}
and
k_{0}
sufficiently small so that

c\varepsilon\leq 1/4,

where the constants
r_{0}
and
k_{0}
depend on
\mathsf{data}
,
{\omega}
and
N
. Thus we now fix

(5.33)

R_{0}=2^{-k_{0}}r_{0}

to see that for any
r\leq R_{0}

(5.34)

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{l}E(u;B_{2^{-m(k+1)}r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\left[E(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|+{\mathrm{Tail}}\left(\frac{u}{r^{s}};B_{r}(z)\right)+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,r)\right]

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},{{\omega}},N)
, where we have used the fact that

{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\rho)\leq\|\omega\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{1/8})}\quad\text{for any }\rho>0.

Using this along with the fact that

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-lm}r}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{-lm}r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{-lm}r}(z)}|\leq\sum_{i=0}^{l}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{-im}r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|,

we obtain that for any
l\geq 0
and
r\leq R_{0}
, there holds

(5.35)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-lm}r}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx\leq c\left({E}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|+{\mathrm{Tail}}(u/r^{s};B_{r}(z))+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,r)\right),

where
c=c(\mathsf{data},{{\omega}},N)
.
We now assume
r>R_{0}
. Let us fix a positive integer
l
. If
2^{-lm}r\geq 2^{-m}R_{0}
, then we have

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-lm}r}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx\leq c\left({E}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},{{\omega}},N)
. If
2^{-lm}r<2^{-m}R_{0}
, then there is a positive integer
i
such that
2^{-(i+1)m}R_{0}\leq 2^{-lm}r<2^{-im}R_{0}
. By (
5.35
), we have

(5.36)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-lm}r}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-im}R_{0}}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq c\left[{E}(u;B_{R_{0}}(z))+c|(u/\phi)_{B_{R_{0}}(z)}|+{\mathrm{Tail}}(u;B_{R_{0}}(z))+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,R_{0})\right]

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},{{\omega}},N)
. Using (
4.7
), (
5.2
) and the fact that
d(z)\leq c(\Omega)
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathrm{Tail}}(u;B_{R_{0}}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\left({\mathrm{Tail}}(u/{r}^{s};B_{r}(z))+\|u\|_{L^{1}(B_{r}(z))}\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\left({E}(u;B_{r}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r}(z)}|+{\mathrm{Tail}}(u/{r}^{s};B_{r}(z))\right).

Plugging this into (
5.36
) together with Lemma
5.2
yields (
5.35
) provided
r\in(R_{0},1/64]
. This completes the proof.
∎

Using this lemma
5.5
, we now prove a pointwise estimate of the maximal function of
u/d^{s}
.

Lemma 5.6
.

Let us assume (
5.21
). Then we have

\displaystyle M^{\Omega}_{1/64}(u/d^{s})(z)\leq c\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+c\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},N,{\boldsymbol{\omega}})
.

Proof.

Let us fix
r\in(0,1/64]
. Then there is a positive integer
i
such that
2^{-im-6}<r\leq 2^{-(i-1)m-6}
. By Lemma
5.5
, we have

(5.37)

\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx\leq c{E}(u;B_{2^{-6}}(z))+c|(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{-6}}(z)}|+c{\mathrm{Tail}}(u;B_{2^{-6}}(z))+c\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-6})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},N,{\boldsymbol{\omega}})
. Moreover, we observe

(5.38)

\displaystyle{E}(u;B_{2^{-6}}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{2^{-6}}(z)}|

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u/\phi\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-6}}(z))}+\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{1}(B_{2^{-6}}(z))}+\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+c\|f\|_{L^{2_{*}}(\Omega\cap B_{2^{-3}}(z))}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},N,{\boldsymbol{\omega}})
, where we have used (
5.2
), (
4.29
) and

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2^{-6}}(z)}\frac{|\phi(y)|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\leq c.

Using this, we further estimate the right-hand side of (
5.37
) as

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\right).

Since this holds for every
r\in(0,1/64]
, we have the desired estimate.
∎

Remark 5.7
.

We note that if
\omega(\rho)\leq L\rho^{\alpha}
for some
L\geq 1
, where
\rho\in[0,1/8]
, then we can choose the constants
r_{0}
and
\varepsilon
given in (
5.26
), where such constants depend only on
\mathsf{data}
,
L
and
\alpha
. Therefore, (
5.22
) holds with the constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha)
.

We now use Lemma
5.5
to obtain a point-wise estimate of the following nonlocal sharp maximal function

(5.39)

{N}^{\sigma}_{1/64}(u;z)\coloneqq\sup_{0<r<1/64}\frac{{E}(u;B_{r}(z))}{r^{\sigma}},

where
\sigma\in(0,\alpha)
and
z\in B_{1/4}
. We note that such a maximal function is first introduced in
[
DN23
]
.

Lemma 5.8
.

Let us assume for any
\rho\in(0,1/8]
,

(5.40)

{{\omega}}(\rho)\leq L\rho^{\alpha},

where
L>0
and
u\equiv 0
in
{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2}
. Then for any
\sigma\in(0,\alpha)
, we have

(5.41)

\displaystyle{N}^{\sigma}_{1/64}(u;z)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,1/4)+\left({M}^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\sigma),1/4}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\right),

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\alpha,L,\sigma)
.

Remark 5.9
.

We note from Remark
5.1
that we always assume
u\equiv 0
in
{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2}
.

Proof.

Let us fix
\rho\in(0,1/2]
which will be determined later. Then for any
r\in(0,\rho/64]
, Lemma
5.4
yields
