\displaystyle{E}(u;B_{r}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\rho^{\alpha}{E}(u;B_{r/\rho}(z))

\displaystyle\quad+c\rho^{-n}({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(r/\rho)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(r/\rho))({E}(u;B_{r/\rho}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r/\rho}(z)}|)

\displaystyle\quad+c\rho^{-n}\max\{\phi(z),(r/\rho)^{s}\}^{-1}{\mathrm{Tail}}(u(\cdot){\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|\cdot-z|);B_{r/\rho}(z))

\displaystyle\quad+c\rho^{-n}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r/\rho}(z)}((r/\rho)^{s}|f|)^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\coloneqq\sum_{i=1}^{4}I_{i},

where
c=c(\mathsf{data})
. We now choose
\rho=\rho(\mathsf{data},\sigma)
such that

(5.42)

c\rho^{\alpha-\sigma}\leq 1/4

to see that

(5.43)

I_{1}\leq\frac{\rho^{\sigma}}{4}E(u;B_{r/\rho}(z)).

By (
5.40
), (
5.9
) and (
5.42
), we have

{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(r/\rho)\leq cr^{\alpha}

and

{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(r/\rho)=(r/\rho)^{s}\int_{r/\rho}^{\infty}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(\xi)}{\xi^{1+s}}\,d\xi\leq(r/\rho)^{s}\int_{r/\rho}^{1/8}\frac{\omega(\xi)}{\xi^{1+s}}\,d\xi\leq c(r/\rho)^{\alpha}\leq cr^{\alpha}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha)
.
Therefore, we have

I_{2}\leq cr^{\alpha}({E}(u;B_{r/\rho}(z))+|(u/\phi)_{B_{r/\rho}(z)}|)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha,\sigma)
. Since
r/\rho\leq 1/64
, there is a positive integer
i
such that

(5.44)

2^{-6-im}\leq r/\rho\leq 2^{-6-(i-1)m}.

By Remark
5.7
, we have for any nonnegative integer
k\in[0,(i-1)m]
,

(5.45)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{k}r/\rho}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\right)

for some
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha,\sigma)
. As in the estimate of
J_{1}
and
J_{2}
given in (
5.18
) and (
5.20
), we get

(5.46)

\displaystyle\max\{\phi(z),(r/\rho)^{s}\}^{-1}\mathrm{Tail}(u-(u/\phi)_{B_{r/\rho}(z)}\phi;B_{r/\rho}(z))

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{k=0}^{(i-1)m}2^{-ks}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{k}r/\rho}(z))

\displaystyle+c2^{-(i-1)ms}E(u;B_{2^{-3}}(z)).

Using (
5.45
), (
5.46
) and (
5.38
), we have

(5.47)

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq cr^{\alpha}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{(i-1)m}2^{-ks}E_{\mathrm{loc}}(u;B_{2^{k}r/\rho}(z))+2^{-(i-1)ms}E(u;B_{2^{-3}}(z))\right)

\displaystyle\leq cr^{\alpha}\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\right),

where
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha,\sigma)
. We now estimate
I_{3}
. By
[
DKLN24a
, Lemma 2.2]
, we have

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq c\rho^{-n}\max\{\phi(z),(r/\rho)^{s}\}^{-1}(r/\rho)^{2s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{r/\rho}(z)}\frac{|u(y)|}{|y-z|^{n+2s-\alpha}}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq c\rho^{-n}\max\{\phi(z),(r/\rho)^{s}\}^{-1}(r/\rho)^{2s}\sum_{k=0}^{(i-1)m}(2^{k}r/\rho)^{-2s+\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{k}r/\rho}(z)}|u|\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+c\rho^{-n}\max\{\phi(z),(r/\rho)^{s}\}^{-1}(r/\rho)^{2s}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-6}}(z)}|u|\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+c\rho^{-n}\max\{\phi(z),(r/\rho)^{s}\}^{-1}(r/\rho)^{2s}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2^{-6}}(z)}\frac{|u(y)|}{|y-z|^{n+2s-\alpha}}\,dy\coloneqq\sum_{j=1}^{3}I_{3,j}.

By (
5.19
), we estimate
I_{3,1}
and
I_{3,2}
as

\displaystyle I_{3,1}+I_{3,2}

\displaystyle\leq cr^{2s}\left[\sum_{k=0}^{(i-1)m}2^{ks}(2^{k}r)^{-2s+\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{k}r/\rho}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dy+2^{ims}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{2^{-6}}(z)}|u/\phi|\,dy\right]

\displaystyle\leq cr^{\alpha}\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha,\sigma)
, where we have used Lemma
5.6
, (
5.37
) and (
5.44
). Thus we have

(5.48)

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq cr^{\sigma}\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha,\sigma)
, where we have used the fact that
u\equiv 0
in
{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2}
and
\sigma<\alpha
. We next estimate
I_{4}
as

(5.49)

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq cr^{\sigma}\left(r^{2_{*}(s-\sigma)}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{r}(z)}|f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\leq cr^{\sigma}\left(M^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\sigma),1/4}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z))\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}},

where
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha,\sigma)
.
Therefore, combining four estimates
I_{1},I_{2},I_{3}
and
I_{4}
given in (
5.43
), (
5.47
), (
5.48
) and (
5.49
), respectively, we discover that for
r\leq\rho/64
,

(5.50)

\displaystyle E(u;B_{r}(z))/r^{\sigma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}E(u;B_{r/\rho}(z))/(r/\rho)^{\sigma}

\displaystyle+c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,1/4)+\left(M^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\sigma),1/4}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z))\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\right)

where
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha,\sigma)
. In addition, for
r>\rho/64
, we note from (
5.38
) that

(5.51)

\displaystyle E(u;B_{r}(z))/r^{\sigma}

\displaystyle\leq cE(u;B_{1/8}(z))\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,1/4)\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha,\sigma)
. To get the desired estimate (
5.41
), we now consider

{N}^{\sigma,\varepsilon}_{2^{-6}}(u;z)\coloneqq\sup_{\varepsilon/8<r<2^{-6}}\frac{{E}(u;B_{r}(z))}{r^{\sigma}}

for any
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
. Then by (
5.50
) and (
5.51
), we have

\displaystyle{N}^{\sigma,\varepsilon}_{2^{-6}}(u;z)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,1/4)+\left(M^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\sigma),1/4}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z))\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},L,\alpha,\sigma)
. By taking
\varepsilon\to 0
, we get (
5.41
).
∎

5.2.
Proof of Main Theorems

In this subsection, we are going to prove our main results for continuous coefficient case, namely Theorem
1.8
, Corollary
1.9
, Theorem
1.12
and Theorem
1.14
.

We start by proving Theorem
1.8
.

Proof of Theorem
1.8
.

We first note that since
A
is Dini-continuous in
B_{1}\times B_{1}
,
A
is also VMO in
B_{1}\times B_{1}
. Therefore, by (
4.7
) and Theorem
1.4
, we have
u\in L^{n/s}(B_{7/8})
with

(5.52)

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{n/s}(B_{7/8})}\leq c\max\{1,d(0)^{s}\}\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{n/(2s)}(B_{7/8})}

\displaystyle\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{{n}/{(2s)}}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

\displaystyle\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{{n}/{s},1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\omega)
. Let us fix
z\in\overline{\Omega}\cap B_{1/2}
. We are going to prove

(5.53)

M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{{n}/{s},1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\omega)
. To do this, we first use Lemma
5.6
to see that

M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}(\widetilde{u}/d^{s})(z)\leq c\|\widetilde{u}\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+c\mathbf{W}^{|F|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2}),

where
\widetilde{u}\in W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})
is a weak solution to (
5.5
).
By the fact that
\widetilde{u}=u
in
B_{5/64}(z)
and
|\widetilde{u}(x)|\leq|u(x)|
, we have

(5.54)

\displaystyle M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}({u}/d^{s})(z)

\displaystyle\leq c\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+c\mathbf{W}^{|F|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\omega)
. It remains to estimate the last term given in the right-hand side of (
5.54
). To this end, we use (
5.6
) to get that

(5.55)

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}^{|F|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\leq c\left[\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})+\mathbf{W}^{|u|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})+\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{5/8}(z))\right]

for some constant
c=c(n,s,\Lambda)
. We first observe from (
2.3
) that

(5.56)

\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\leq c\||f|^{2_{*}}\|_{L^{n/(2_{*}s),1/2_{*}}(\Omega\cap B_{7/8})}\leq c\|f\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}

for some constant
c=c(n,s)
, where we have used a simple property of Lorentz space for the last inequality. On the other hand, by (
5.52
), we have

(5.57)

\mathbf{W}^{|u|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\leq c\|u\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{7/8})}\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\omega)
. Combining (
5.55
), (
5.56
) and (
5.57
) gives

(5.58)

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}^{|F|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-3})\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\omega)
. Plugging this into (
5.54
) yields

\displaystyle M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}({u}/d^{s})(z)

\displaystyle\leq c\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+c\|f\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}),

where
c=c(\mathsf{data},\omega)
. Since
z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/2}
was arbitrary, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/2}}M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}({u}/d^{s})(z)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}\right),

which implies the desired estimate.
∎

Using Theorem
1.8
, we now prove Corollary

1.9
.

Proof of Corollary
1.9
.

Let
x,y\in B_{1/2}
. We distinguish the following cases to complete the proof.

Case (a)
: Suppose

5|x-y|\geq\max\{d(x),d(y)\}.

Then by Theorem
1.8
, we have

(5.59)

\displaystyle|u(x)-u(y)|

\displaystyle\leq|u(x)|+|u(y)|

\displaystyle\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})(|d^{s}(x)|+|d^{s}(y)|)

\displaystyle\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})|x-y|^{s}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.

Case (b)
: Suppose

5|x-y|\leq\max\{d(x),d(y)\}.

We may assume
d(x)\geq d(y)
. Then
y\in B_{d(x)}(x)\subset\Omega
. Since
A
is VMO in
B_{1}\times B_{1}
and
f\in L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})\subset L^{n/s,\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{1})
, we note from
[
DN23
, Theorem 1.7]
that

(5.60)

\frac{|u(x)-u(y)|}{|x-y|^{s}}\leq\frac{c}{d^{s}(x)}\left(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{2d(x)/3}(x))}+\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{2d(x)/3}(x))+d^{s}(x)\|f\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{d(x)}(x))}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\omega)
.
Let us fix a positive integer
l
such that

(5.61)

1/16\leq 2^{l}d(x)<1/8\quad\text{and}\quad B_{2^{l}d(x)}(x)\subset B_{3/4}.

By (
5.19
) together with (
5.2
), there is a constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
such that

\displaystyle|d(y)|\leq c2^{k}d(x)\quad\text{for any }y\in B_{2^{k}d(x)}(x).

Thus we have

\|u\|_{L^{\infty}\left(B_{2^{k}d(x)}(x)\right)}\leq cd^{s}(x)2^{ks}\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}\left(B_{2^{k}d(x)}(x)\right)}\leq cd^{s}(x)2^{ks}\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\Omega\cap B_{1/8}(x)\right)}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Therefore, using this,
[
DKLN24a
, Lemma 2.2]
and (
5.61
), we get

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{2d(x)/3}(x))}+\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{2d(x)/3}(x))

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\sum_{i=1}^{l}2^{-2si}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}\left(B_{2^{i}d(x)}(x)\right)}+2^{-2sl}\mathrm{Tail}(u;B_{1/8}(x))\right]

\displaystyle\leq cd(x)^{s}(\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{1/8}(x))}+\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}).

Plugging this into (
5.60
) together with Theorem
1.8
yields

(5.62)

\displaystyle\frac{|u(x)-u(y)|}{|x-y|^{s}}\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
The desired estimate then follows from (
5.59
) and (
5.62
).
∎

We next prove Theorem
1.12
and Theorem
1.14
.

Proof of Theorem
1.12
.

Let us fix
\sigma\in(0,\alpha)
and
q\in[2_{*},n/(s-\sigma))
. Let us denote

\widetilde{\sigma}\coloneqq\frac{\sigma+\alpha}{2}<\alpha.

We now claim that for any
z\in\overline{\Omega}\cap B_{1/2}
, there holds

(5.63)

\displaystyle M^{\#,\Omega}_{\widetilde{\sigma},2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)\right)

\displaystyle+c\left(\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})+\mathbf{W}^{|u|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\right)

\displaystyle+c\left(\left(M^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\widetilde{\sigma}),2^{-2}}(|u|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+\left(M^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\widetilde{\sigma}),2^{-2}}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},q,\sigma,K)
. To this end, we fix
z\in\overline{\Omega}\cap B_{1/2}
. By (
1.9
), for any
\delta>0
, there is a constant
\rho_{0}=\rho_{0}(\alpha,K)
such that
A
is
(\delta,\rho_{0})
-vanishing.
Thus by Theorem
1.4
along with (
4.7
), we have

(5.64)

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-(s-\widetilde{\sigma})q}}(B_{7/8})}\leq c\max\{1,d^{s}(0)\}\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-(s-\widetilde{\sigma})q}}(B_{7/8})}

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n+\widetilde{\sigma}q}}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}\right]

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}\right]

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},K)
. We next employ Remark
5.1
to observe that there is a weak solution
\widetilde{u}\in W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})
to (
5.5
) and (
5.9
) with
\widetilde{u}\equiv 0
in
{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2}
. We note from (
1.9
) that (
5.40
) holds with
L=K
. Therefore, Lemma
5.8
yields

(5.65)

\displaystyle{N}^{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{2^{-6}}(\widetilde{u};z)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|\widetilde{u}\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\mathbf{W}^{|F|^{2_{*}}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})+\left({M}^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\widetilde{\sigma}),2^{-2}}(|F|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\right),

where
c=c(\mathsf{data},K,\sigma)
. For any
x,y\in\overline{\Omega}\cap B_{2^{-6}}(z)
, we observe

\displaystyle|(u/d^{s})(x)-(u/d^{s})(y)|

\displaystyle=|(\widetilde{u}/d^{s})(x)-(\widetilde{u}/d^{s})(y)|

\displaystyle\leq|(\widetilde{u}/\phi)(x)-(\widetilde{u}/\phi)(y)||(\phi/d^{s})(x)|+|(\widetilde{u}/\phi)(y)||(\phi/d^{s})(x)-(\phi/d^{s})(y)|

\displaystyle\leq c|(\widetilde{u}/\phi)(x)-(\widetilde{u}/\phi)(y)|+c|(\widetilde{u}/\phi)(y)||x-y|^{\widetilde{\sigma}}

\displaystyle\leq c|(\widetilde{u}/\phi)(x)-(\widetilde{u}/\phi)(y)|+c|({u}/d^{s})(y)||x-y|^{\widetilde{\sigma}}

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\sigma)
, where we have used the third condition given in (
5.5
), (
5.2
) and (
5.4
). Combining this and (
5.65
), we get

(5.66)

\displaystyle M^{\#,\Omega}_{\widetilde{\sigma},2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)

\displaystyle\leq c\left({N}^{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{2^{-6}}(\widetilde{u};z)+M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}({u}/d^{s})(z)\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{2^{-3}}(z)}|f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}({u}/d^{s})(z)\right)

\displaystyle+c\left(\mathbf{W}^{|F|^{2_{*}}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})+\left({M}^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\widetilde{\sigma}),2^{-2}}(|F|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},K,\sigma)
.
As in the estimate of (
5.55
) along with (
5.6
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}^{|F|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})+\left({M}^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\widetilde{\sigma}),2^{-2}}(|F|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})+\mathbf{W}^{|u|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-2})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+c\left(\left({M}^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\widetilde{\sigma}),2^{-2}}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+\left({M}^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-\widetilde{\sigma}),2^{-2}}(|u|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\sigma)
. Combining this and (
5.66
) proves the claim (
5.63
).
We now employ the standard strong p-p estimate and (
5.64
) to see that

\displaystyle\|M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-(s-\widetilde{\sigma}q)}}(B_{1/2})}\leq c\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-(s-\widetilde{\sigma}q)}}(B_{3/4})}\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}).

Plugging this into (
5.63
) and applying (
2.2
) along with (
2.4
) to the terms on the second and third lines in (
5.63
), we have

\displaystyle\|M^{\#,\Omega}_{\widetilde{\sigma},2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-(s-\widetilde{\sigma})q}}(B_{1/2})}\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},K,\sigma,q)
. We now apply Lemma
2.2
with
p=\frac{nq}{n-(s-\widetilde{\sigma})q}
,
\beta=\widetilde{\sigma}
and
t=\sigma
to see that

\displaystyle\|u/d^{s}\|_{W^{\sigma,\frac{nq}{n-(s-\sigma)q}}(B_{2^{-7}})}

\displaystyle\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{\frac{nq}{n-(s-\widetilde{\sigma})q}}(B_{2^{-6}})}+\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

\displaystyle\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},K,\sigma,q)
, where we have used (
5.64
) for the last inequality. By the standard covering argument, we obtain the desired estimate.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.14
.

Let us fix
\sigma\in(0,\alpha)
. As in the proof of (
5.63
), we have

(5.67)

\displaystyle M^{\#,\Omega}_{{\sigma},2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega\cap B_{1}}|f|^{2_{*}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+cM^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)\right)

\displaystyle+c\left(\mathbf{W}^{|f|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-3})+\mathbf{W}^{|u|^{2_{*}}\mbox{\Large$\chi$}_{\Omega}}_{\frac{2_{*}s}{2_{*}+1},2_{*}+1}(z,2^{-3})\right)

\displaystyle+c\left(\left(M^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-{\sigma}),2^{-3}}(|u|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}+\left(M^{\Omega}_{2_{*}(s-{\sigma}),2^{-3}}(|f|^{2_{*}})(z)\right)^{\frac{1}{2_{*}}}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},K,\sigma)
.
Since
A
is Hölder continuous and
f\in L^{n/(s-\sigma),\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{1})\subset L^{n/s,1}(\Omega\cap B_{1})
, we have

(5.68)

\displaystyle\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{3/4})}\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/(s-\sigma),\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{1})})

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},K,\sigma)
, which implies

\|M^{\Omega}_{2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{1/2})}\leq\|u/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{3/4})}\leq c(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/(s-\sigma),\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}).

Using this and applying (
2.3
) and (
2.5
) into second and third lines in (
5.67
), we obtain

\displaystyle M^{\#,\Omega}_{{\sigma},2^{-6}}(u/d^{s})(z)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|u\|_{L^{n/(s-\sigma)}(\Omega\cap B_{3/4})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/(s-\sigma)}(\Omega\cap B_{3/4})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/(s-\sigma)}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data},\sigma,K)
, whenever
z\in B_{1/2}
. Therefore, using this and
[
Giu03
, Theorem 2.9]
together with the fact that
\Omega
is a
C^{1,\alpha}
-domain, we have

\displaystyle\|u/d^{s}\|_{C^{\sigma}(\overline{\Omega}\cap B_{1/2})}\leq c\left(\|u\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{n/(s-\sigma)}(\Omega\cap B_{1})}\right),

which completes the proof.
∎

We end this section by providing some details on the validity of our results to more general kernel coefficients which are comparable to the recent work
[
KW24a
]
in the case of the Hölder continuity assumption.

5.3.
General kernel coefficients

In this subsection, we apply our arguments to obtain the same estimates as in Theorem
1.4
, Theorem
1.8
, Corollary
1.9
, Theorem
1.12
and Theorem
1.14
with more general kernel coefficients.

Let us fix
\rho_{0}>0
. We then assume that the associated kernel coefficient
A
satisfies

(5.69)

\sup_{0<R\leq\rho_{0}}\sup_{z\in B_{1}}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{R}(z)}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{R}(z)}|A-\overline{A}_{B_{R}(z)}(x,y)|\,dx\,dy\leq\delta,

where

\overline{A}_{B_{R}(z)}(x,y)\coloneqq\frac{1}{2}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{R}(z)}(A(x-y+h,h)+A(y-x+h,h))\,dh\quad\text{for }x,y\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}.

Since the function
\overline{A}_{B_{R}(z)}(x,y)
is translation invariant, we first note from
[
RW24
, Theorem 1.4 & Theorem 1.6]
that any weak solution
v
to

\left\{\begin{aligned} \mathcal{L}_{\overline{A}_{B_{2r}(z)}}{v}&=f&&\qquad\mbox{in $\Omega\cap B_{r}(z)$},\\
{v}&=0&&\qquad\mbox{in $B_{r}(z)\setminus\Omega$},\end{aligned}\right.

satisfies

r^{s}\|v/d^{s}\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{r/2}(z))}\leq c\left(\|v\|_{L^{1}_{2s}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\cap B_{r}(z))}\right)

for some constant
c=c(\mathsf{data})
.
Therefore, we obtain the estimate given in Lemma
4.2
when

\displaystyle a_{0}(x,y)\coloneqq\begin{cases}\overline{A}_{B_{2r}(z)}(x,y)&\text{if }(x,y)\in B_{2r}(z)\times B_{2r}(z),\\
A(x,y)&\text{if }(x,y)\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2n}\setminus(B_{2r}(z)\times B_{2r}(z)).\end{cases}

By following the same lines as in the proof of Lemmas
4.3
-
4.7
with
(A)_{B_{2r}(z)\times B_{2r}(z)}
replaced by
\overline{A}_{B_{2r}(z)}
, we prove the same result in Theorem
1.4
when the vanishing condition given in Definition
1.3
is replaced by (
5.69
).

We now consider the case of continuous kernel coefficients.
Let us assume that the kernel coefficient
A
satisfies

(5.70)

\sup_{0<|h|<1}\sup_{x,y\in B_{1}}|A(x+h,y+h)-A(x,y)|\leq\omega(|h|),

where
\omega:{\mathbb{R}}_{+}\to{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
is a non-decreasing function with
\omega(0)=0
. We first note that if
A
satisfies (
5.70
), then there is a sufficiently small
\rho_{0}=\rho_{0}(\omega)
such that
A
satisfies the vanishing condition (
5.69
).
By
[
RW24
, Theorem 1.1, Theorem 1.4 & Theorem 1.6]
, we next observe that for any
z\in\Omega\cap B_{1/2}
, there is the weak solution
\phi\equiv\phi_{z}
to (
5.1
) with

(5.71)

A_{z}(x,y)\coloneqq(A(x-y+z,z)+A(y-x+z,z))/2\quad\text{for }x,y\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n},

satisfying (
5.2
) and (
5.3
). Since we can apply Lemma
2.3
with
a=A_{z}
there, to obtain Remark
5.1
, we now consider a weak solution
u\in W^{s,2}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})
to (
5.7
) with

|A(x,y)-A_{z}(x,y)|\leq({\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|y-z|)+{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|x-z|))/2,

where
\boldsymbol{\omega}
satisfies the same condition as in (
5.9
).
By following the same lines as in the proof of
[
KW24a
, Lemma 6.6]
together with
[
RW24
, Theorem 1.1,Theorem 1.4 & Theorem 1.6]
, the weak solution
v
to (
3.5
) with
a_{0}=A_{z}
satisfies (
5.11
) with
\alpha
replaced by
\alpha s/2
. In addition, we have

\displaystyle r^{4s}\left(\sup_{x\in B_{r}(z)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{|A-A_{z}|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq r^{4s}\left(\sup_{x\in B_{r}(z)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{\boldsymbol{\omega}(|y-z|)+\boldsymbol{\omega}(|x-z|)}{2|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq r^{4s}\left(\sup_{x\in B_{r}(z)}\int_{B_{1/8}(z)\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{{\omega}(|y-z|)}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq r^{4s}\left(\int_{2r}^{1/8}\frac{{\omega}(\rho)}{\rho^{1+2s}}\,dy\right)^{2}\leq{\boldsymbol{\omega}}_{G}(2r)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in B_{r}(z)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{|w(y)||A-A_{z}|}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq\int_{B_{1/8}(z)\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{|w(y)|(\boldsymbol{\omega}(|y-z|)+\boldsymbol{\omega}(|x-z|))}{2|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq\int_{B_{1/8}(z)\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{|w(y)|{\omega}(|y-z|)}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\setminus B_{2r}(z)}\frac{|w(y)|{\boldsymbol{\omega}}(|y-z|)}{|y-z|^{n+2s}}\,dy.

Thus, we are able to prove (
5.12
) and Lemma
5.4
with
\alpha
replaced by
\alpha s/2
. Using this along with (
5.2
) and (
5.3
), we can prove Lemma
5.5
and Lemma
5.6
when

(5.72)

\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\omega(\rho)}{\rho}\,d\rho<\infty.

Finally, all results given in Theorem
1.8
and Corollary
1.9
hold if
A
satisfies (
5.70
) and (
5.72
).

However, to get a higher differentiability of
u/d^{s}
, we need to impose more regularity assumption on the frozen kernel
A_{z}
.
To do this, we now assume that
A
satisfies (
5.70
) with

(5.73)

{\omega(\rho)}\leq K\rho^{\alpha},

and the frozen kernel coefficient
A_{z}
given in (
5.71
) is homogeneous for any
z\in B_{1}
(see
[
FR24
, Definition 2.1.21]
for a precise definition of the homogeneity of the kernel coefficient). We first observe from
[
FR24
]
that (
5.2
), (
5.3
) and (
5.4
) hold with
A(z,z)
replaced by
A_{z}
.
By
[
KW24a
, Lemma 8.1]
, we obtain the same estimates as in Lemma
5.4
. Since under the assumption (
5.73
), (
5.72
) immediately follows. Therefore, we are able to prove Lemma
5.8
. Using this together with (
5.2
), (
5.3
) and (
5.4
), we finally prove Theorem
1.12
and Theorem
1.14
.

Appendix A
Some sharpness results

In this appendix, we construct some examples to establish the sharpness of the estimates of Theorem
1.4
, Theorem
1.12
and Theorem
1.14
.

Let us take a smooth domain
\Omega
such that

\Omega\cap B_{1}=B_{1}^{+}.

We take

f(x)\coloneqq|x|^{-n/q}

for any
q>1
. We have

u(x)=\int_{\Omega}G(x,y)f(y)\,dy

is a weak solution to (
1.6
) with
A=1
, where
G
is the green function with respect to the domain
\Omega
. By
[
CKS10
]
, we have for any
x\in B_{1}^{+}
,

\displaystyle u(x)

\displaystyle\geq\int_{B_{1}^{+}}\min\left\{\frac{x_{n}}{|x-y|},1\right\}^{s}\min\left\{\frac{y_{n}}{|x-y|},1\right\}^{s}\frac{|y|^{-n/q}}{|x-y|^{n-2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\geq\int_{B_{x_{n}}(x)}\min\left\{\frac{x_{n}}{|x-y|},1\right\}^{s}\min\left\{\frac{y_{n}}{|x-y|},1\right\}^{s}\frac{|y|^{-n/q}}{|x-y|^{n-2s}}\,dy.

Since for any
y\in B^{+}_{x_{n}}(x)\coloneqq\{(z^{\prime},z_{n})\in B_{x_{n}}(x)\,:\,z_{n}\geq x_{n}\}

\min\left\{\frac{x_{n}}{|x-y|},1\right\}\leq 1\quad\text{and}\quad\min\left\{\frac{y_{n}}{|x-y|},1\right\}\leq 2,

we have

\displaystyle u(x)\geq\int_{B^{+}_{x_{n}}(x)}\frac{|y|^{-n/q}}{|x-y|^{n-2s}}\,dy\geq\int_{B^{+}_{x_{n}}(x)\cap|y|\geq\frac{|x|}{2}}\frac{|y|^{-n/q}}{|x-y|^{n-2s}}\,dy.

We observe from

B^{+}_{x_{n}}(x)\cap|y|\geq\frac{|x|}{2}=\{x+x_{n}\theta\,:\,\theta\in S_{n}^{+}\quad\text{and}\quad|x+x_{n}\theta|\geq|x|/2\}

that there is a set
A\subset S_{n}^{+}
such that
B^{+}_{x_{n}}(x)\cap|y|=\{x+x_{n}\theta\,:\,\theta\in A\}
and

\int_{A}\,dS\geq c|S_{n}^{+}|

for some constant
c
, where
dS
is the surface measure on
S_{n}^{+}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle u(x)\geq\int_{B^{+}_{x_{n}}(x)}\frac{|y|^{-n/q}}{|x-y|^{n-2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle\geq\int_{B^{+}_{x_{n}}(x)\cap|y|\geq\frac{|x|}{2}}\frac{|x|^{-n/q}}{|x-y|^{n-2s}}\,dy

\displaystyle=c|x|^{-n/q}\int_{0}^{x_{n}}\int_{A}\frac{1}{r^{1-2s}}\,dS\,dr\geq c|x|^{-n/q}x_{n}^{2s}

for some constant
c=c(n,s)
. Therefore, we have

(A.1)

|u/d^{s}(x)|\geq c|x|^{-n/q}x_{n}^{s}\quad\text{in }B_{1}^{+}

and

\displaystyle\int_{B_{1}^{+}}|u/d^{s}(x)|^{p}\,dx\geq c\int_{B_{1}^{+}}\left||x|^{-n/q}x_{n}^{s}\right|^{p}\,dx

\displaystyle\geq c\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S^{+}_{n}}\left|r^{-n/q}r^{s}\theta_{n}^{s}\right|^{p}r^{n-1}\,dS\,dr

\displaystyle\geq c\int_{0}^{1}r^{(s-n/q)p-(n-1)}\,dr.

This implies
u/d^{s}\notin L^{p}
if
p\geq\frac{nq}{n-sq}
but
u/d^{s}\in L^{p}
if
p<\frac{nq}{n-sq}
. We now fix
q\in[2_{*},n/s)
. Then we have that for any
\varepsilon\in(0,n/s-q)
there is a weak solution
u
to (
1.5
) with
f(x)=|x|^{-\frac{n\left(1+\frac{\varepsilon s(n-sq)}{2n^{2}}\right)}{q+\frac{\varepsilon(n-sq)}{2n}}}\in L^{q}(\Omega\cap B_{1})
, but

u/d^{s}\notin L^{\frac{nq}{n-sq}+\varepsilon}(\Omega\cap B_{1}).
